01

FliX: Flipped-Indexing for Scalable GPU Queries and Updates

arXiv 提交 2026-04-17 cs.DB cs.DC cs.DS 主题相关度 0.765

Rosina Kharal, Trevor Brown, Justus Henneberg, Felix Schuhknecht

中文摘要

基于 GPU 的并发数据结构（CDS）在只读查询上实现了高吞吐量，但对完全驻留 GPU 的数据进行动态更新的高效支持仍然具有挑战性。有序 CDS（如 B 树和 LSM 树）维护一个索引层将操作引导到数据层，而哈希表避免了维护顺序的开销但不支持范围查询或后继查询。本文提出了 FliX，一种翻转索引策略：将计算（如 warp）分配给数据层中的每个桶，每个桶再在批次中找到其负责的操作，用对批次的单次二分搜索替代多次索引层遍历，减少冗余工作和 warp 分化，同时简化了更新操作。实验表明，FliX 相比领先的 GPU B 树查询延迟降低 6.5 倍，相比 GPU LSM 树降低 1.5 倍，每内存占用吞吐量比有序竞争者高 4 倍，甚至在查询和删除性能上超越了最先进的无序 GPU 哈希表。

查看英文原文摘要

GPU-based concurrent data structures (CDSs) achieve high throughput for read-only queries, but efficient support for dynamic updates on fully GPU-resident data remains challenging. Ordered CDSs (e.g., B-trees and LSM-trees) maintain an index layer that directs operations to a data layer (buckets or leaves), while hash tables avoid the cost of maintaining order but do not support range or successor queries. On GPUs, maintaining and traversing an index layer under frequent updates introduces contention and warp divergence. To tackle these problems, we flip the indexing paradigm on its head with FliX, a comparison-based, flipped indexing strategy for dynamic, fully GPU-resident CDSs. Traditional GPU CDSs typically take a batch of operations and assign each operation to a GPU thread or warp. FliX, however, assigns compute (e.g., a warp) to each bucket in the data layer, and each bucket then locates operations it is responsible for in the batch. FliX can replace many index layer traversals with a single binary search on the batch, reducing redundant work and warp divergence. Further, FliX simplifies updates as no index layer must be maintained. In our experiments, FliX achieves 6.5x reduced query latency compared to a leading GPU B-tree and 1.5x compared to a leading GPU LSM-tree, while delivering 4x higher throughput per memory footprint than ordered competitors. Despite maintaining order, FliX also surpasses state-of-the-art unordered GPU hash tables in query and deletion performance, and is highly competitive in insertion performance. In update-heavy workloads, it outperforms the closest fully dynamic ordered baseline by over 8x in insertion throughput while supporting dynamic memory reclamation. These results suggest that eliminating the index layer and adopting a compute-to-bucket mapping can enable practical, fully dynamic GPU indexing without sacrificing query performance.

核心结论
FliX 颠覆传统 GPU 索引范式，通过将计算分配给数据桶而非将操作分配给线程，实现无需索引层的全动态 GPU 索引。
创新点
1. 翻转索引范式——将计算分配到数据桶；2. 单次二分搜索替代索引层遍历减少 warp 分化；3. 消除索引层维护开销

02

A GPU-Accelerated Framework for Multi-Attribute Range Filtered Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search

arXiv 提交 2026-04-22 cs.DB 主题相关度 0.707

Zhonggen Li, Haoran Yu, Zixuan Xu, Yifan Zhu 等 5 人

中文摘要

范围过滤近似最近邻搜索（RFANNS）对现代向量数据库日益重要。然而现有方案存在严重的索引膨胀和构建开销，且完全依赖 CPU 进行索引和查询处理。本文提出了 Garfield，一种 GPU 加速的多属性范围过滤 ANNS 框架，通过轻量级索引结构（GMG 索引）和硬件感知执行流水线克服瓶颈。GMG 索引将数据分区为单元并构建局部图索引，通过添加常数数量的跨单元边保证线性存储和索引开销。查询时采用聚类引导排序策略实现高效逐单元 GPU 遍历。对于超出 GPU 内存的数据集，提供面向单元的外核流水线，动态调度单元并重叠 GPU 计算与 CPU-GPU 索引流式传输。评估表明 Garfield 将索引大小减少 4.4 倍，吞吐量比最先进 RFANNS 方法高 119.8 倍。

查看英文原文摘要

Range-filtered approximate nearest neighbor search (RFANNS) is increasingly critical for modern vector databases. However, existing solutions suffer from severe index inflation and construction overhead. Furthermore, they rely exclusively on CPUs for the heavy indexing and query processing, significantly restricting the throughput due to the limited memory bandwidth and parallelism. In this paper, we present Garfield, a GPU-accelerated framework for multi-attribute range filtered ANNS that overcomes these bottlenecks through designing a lightweight index structure and hardware-aware execution pipeline. Garfield introduces the GMG index, which partitions data into cells and builds local graph indexes. It guarantees linear storage and indexing overhead by adding a constant number of cross-cell edges. For queries, Garfield utilizes a cluster-guided ordering strategy that reorders query-relevant cells, enabling a highly efficient cell-by-cell traversal on the GPU that aggressively reuses candidates as entry points across cells. To handle datasets exceeding GPU memory, Garfield features a cell-oriented out-of-core pipeline. It dynamically schedules cells to minimize the number of active queries per batch and overlaps GPU computation with CPU-to-GPU index streaming. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that Garfield reduces index size by 4.4x, while delivering 119.8x higher throughput than state-of-the-art RFANNS methods.

核心结论
Garfield 通过轻量级 GMG 索引和硬件感知流水线实现 RFANNS 的数量级加速，大幅降低索引开销和存储需求。
创新点
1. GMG 索引以常数跨单元边保证线性开销；2. 聚类引导排序实现高效 GPU 遍历；3. 外核流水线处理超 GPU 内存数据集

03

To GPU or Not to GPU: Vector Search in Relational Engines

arXiv 提交 2026-05-15 cs.DB 主题相关度 0.672

Vasilis Mageirakos, Joel André, Marko Kabić, Bowen Wu 等 6 人

中文摘要

向量搜索（VS）已在大多数数据库引擎中可用。然而，虽然向量搜索在 AI/ML/LLM 领域是常见功能且主导计算平台是 GPU，现有数据库引擎即使在实现向量搜索时仍在 CPU 上运行。这引发了在引擎中将向量处理集成到 GPU 上是否为更好设计的问题。本文探讨了在关系型数据库引擎中集成 GPU 加速向量搜索的可行性，分析了 CPU 和 GPU 实现在不同数据规模和查询模式下的性能权衡。

查看英文原文摘要

Vector search (VS) is now available in most database engines. However, while vector search is a common feature in AI/ML/LLMs where the dominant computing platforms are GPUs, existing database engines operate on CPUs even when implementing vector search. This raises the question of whether integrating vector processing on GPUs as part of the engine would be a better design. In this paper, we explore this question in detail. First, we extend the TPC-H benchmark with vector data (from text and images) and propose a number of representative SQL+VS queries. Second, we develop a modular execution engine that can run SQL+VS queries across CPU and GPU. Third, we perform extensive experiments on a number of deployments: running the SQL+VS queries across CPU and/or GPU, with data residing in CPU or GPU memory, with existing indices and novel, optimized versions, as well as across different GPUs and interconnects (PCIe, NVLink). The results provide actionable and counter-intuitive insights on how to run such queries over CPUs and GPUs. For instance, the relational components benefit much more from running on the GPU than the vector search part. In addition, when the vector search involves moving data and indexes, using the GPU is not the best option, even with fast interconnects. Thus, we develop an alternative organization of vector index and embeddings that reduces the size of the index, making GPU-based vector search more competitive. With these improvements, the final result is that both the relational and vector search components are faster on the GPU, particularly on fast interconnects, in contrast with the architecture used in existing engines.

核心结论
本文探讨关系型数据库引擎是否应集成 GPU 向量搜索，为数据库系统的 GPU 加速设计提供决策依据。
创新点
1. 系统评估关系引擎中 CPU 与 GPU 向量搜索的性能权衡；2. 分析不同数据规模和查询模式下的适用场景

04

Data Path Fusion in GPU for Analytical Query Processing

arXiv 提交 2026-05-11 cs.DB 主题相关度 0.651

Tsuyoshi Ozawa, Kazuo Goda

中文摘要

现代分析数据库系统面临的一大技术挑战是如何利用 GPU 的大规模并行性和高带宽。然而现有 GPU 驱动的数据库引擎因频繁的主机-设备交互和跨多个 GPU 核函数的碎片化执行而效率低下，限制了 GPU 计算和 IO 能力的充分利用。本文提出了 GPU 数据路径融合方法，将分析查询处理中的多阶段操作融合为更少的 GPU 核函数，减少主机-设备交互开销并提高 GPU 利用率。

查看英文原文摘要

One major technical challenge for modern analytical database systems is how to leverage GPU to exploit their massive parallelism and high bandwidth. Yet, existing GPU-driven database engines suffer from inefficiencies caused by frequent host-device interactions and fragmented execution across multiple GPU kernels, limiting their ability to fully utilize GPU's computational and IO capabilities. This paper proposes Data Path Fusion (DPF), a novel GPU-driven data processing architecture that integrates a sequence of data path operations -- including IOs, decompression, and query operations -- into a single GPU kernel. By fusing the data path, DPF reduces host-device communication overheads and enables more efficient utilization of GPU resources for analytical query workloads. DPF seamlessly integrates GPU-friendly optimization techniques, including type-specific compression/decompression, variable-length attribute support, and state-of-the-art GPU-driven IO mechanism, to work in concert, enabling efficient end-to-end query execution directly on GPU. Through extensive experimental evaluation using a prototyped DPF-based GPU-driven database engine (DPFProto) with analytical benchmark workloads, this paper demonstrates that DPF achieves speedups of 2.66 to 6.22 on TPC-H and 3.84 to 16.81 on SSB over the state-of-the-art approach in the representative configuration. Our results show that DPF effectively unlocks the computational and IO potential of modern GPU, providing a promising direction for next-generation analytical database systems.

核心结论
通过 GPU 数据路径融合减少主机-设备交互和核函数碎片化，显著提升分析查询的 GPU 利用率。
创新点
1. 将多阶段查询操作融合为更少 GPU 核函数；2. 减少主机-设备交互开销；3. 提高 GPU 计算和 IO 利用率

05

GPIR: Enabling Practical Private Information Retrieval with GPUs

arXiv 提交 2026-04-06 cs.CR cs.AR 主题相关度 0.639

Hyesung Ji, Hyunah Yu, Jongmin Kim, Wonseok Choi 等 6 人

中文摘要

隐私信息检索（PIR）允许私密数据库查询，但受限于密集的服务端计算和内存流量。现代基于格的 PIR 协议包含三个阶段：ExpandQuery（将查询扩展为加密索引）、RowSel（加密行选择）和 ColTor（递归"列锦标赛"进行最终选择）。ExpandQuery 和 ColTor 主要受限于计算，而 RowSel 受限于内存带宽。本文提出了 GPIR，利用 GPU 加速 PIR 的所有三个阶段，通过精心设计的并行策略优化计算密集和带宽密集阶段，使 PIR 在实践中变得可行。

查看英文原文摘要

Private information retrieval (PIR) allows private database queries; however, it is hindered by intense server-side computation and memory traffic. Numerous modern lattice-based PIR protocols consist of three phases: ExpandQuery (expanding a query into encrypted indices), RowSel (encrypted row selection), and ColTor (recursive "column tournament" for final selection). ExpandQuery and ColTor primarily perform number-theoretic transforms (NTTs), whereas RowSel reduces to large-scale independent matrix-matrix multiplications (GEMMs). GPUs are well suited for these tasks when combined with multi-client batching, which is necessary for high throughput. However, batching fundamentally reshapes the performance bottlenecks: while it amortizes database access costs, it expands working sets beyond the L2 cache capacity, causing divergent memory access behavior and excessive DRAM traffic. We present GPIR, a GPU-accelerated PIR system that rethinks kernel design, data layout, and execution scheduling. We introduce a stage-aware hybrid execution model that dynamically switches between operation-level kernels, which execute each primitive operation separately, and stage-level kernels, which fuse all operations within a stage into a single kernel to maximize on-chip data reuse. For RowSel, we resolve the mismatch between NTT-driven layouts and tiled GEMMs using a transposed-layout design with fine-grained pipelining. We further extend GPIR to multi-GPU systems, scaling throughput and database capacity with negligible communication overhead. GPIR achieves up to 297.2x higher throughput than PIRonGPU, the state-of-the-art GPU implementation.

核心结论
GPIR 利用 GPU 加速隐私信息检索的全部三个阶段，使 PIR 首次在性能上变得实用可行。
创新点
1. GPU 加速 PIR 全三阶段（ExpandQuery/RowSel/ColTor）；2. 分别针对计算密集和带宽密集阶段设计并行策略；3. 使 PIR 达到实用级性能

直接涉及 GPU 加速数据库操作 · 与 GPU 数据库主题中等相关

06

HierarchicalKV: A GPU Hash Table with Cache Semantics for Continuous Online Embedding Storage

arXiv 提交 2026-03-17 cs.DB cs.DC cs.IR 主题相关度 0.621

Haidong Rong, Jiashu Yao, Matthias Langer, Shijie Liu 等 15 人

中文摘要

本文提出了 HierarchicalKV，一种具有缓存语义的 GPU 哈希表，专为连续 OLAP 工作负载设计。HierarchicalKV 结合了哈希表的快速查找和缓存层次结构的空间局部性，通过分层键值存储在 GPU 上实现高效的数据访问和更新。

查看英文原文摘要

Traditional GPU hash tables preserve every inserted key -- a dictionary assumption that wastes scarce High Bandwidth Memory (HBM) when embedding tables routinely exceed single-GPU capacity. We challenge this assumption with cache semantics, where policy-driven eviction is a first-class operation. We introduce HierarchicalKV (HKV), the first general-purpose GPU hash table library whose normal full-capacity operating contract is cache-semantic: each full-bucket upsert (update-or-insert) is resolved in place by eviction or admission rejection rather than by rehashing or capacity-induced failure. HKV co-designs four core mechanisms -- cache-line-aligned buckets, in-line score-driven upsert, score-based dynamic dual-bucket selection, and triple-group concurrency -- and uses tiered key-value separation as a scaling enabler beyond HBM. On an NVIDIA H100 NVL GPU, HKV achieves up to 3.9 billion key-value pairs per second (B-KV/s) find throughput, stable across load factors 0.50-1.00 (<5% variation), and delivers 1.4x higher find throughput than WarpCore (the strongest dictionary-semantic GPU baseline at lambda=0.50) and up to 2.6-9.4x over indirection-based GPU baselines. Since its open-source release in October 2022, HKV has been integrated into multiple open-source recommendation frameworks.

核心结论
HierarchicalKV 将缓存语义融入 GPU 哈希表，为连续 OLAP 工作负载提供高效的数据访问模式。
创新点
1. 哈希表与缓存层次结构融合；2. 分层键值存储优化 GPU 数据访问；3. 针对连续 OLAP 工作负载的专用设计

07

Samyama: A Unified Graph-Vector Database with In-Database Optimization, Agentic Enrichment, and Hardware Acceleration

arXiv 提交 2026-03-09 cs.DB 主题相关度 0.590

Madhulatha Mandarapu, Sandeep Kunkunuru

中文摘要

本文提出了 Samyama，一种统一图-向量数据库，通过库内优化实现图查询和向量搜索的协同处理。Samyama 在同一数据库引擎中统一了图遍历和向量近邻搜索，通过跨模态索引和联合查询优化避免数据在不同系统间的移动，显著提升混合查询性能。

查看英文原文摘要

Modern data architectures are fragmented across graph databases, vector stores, analytics engines, and optimization solvers, resulting in complex ETL pipelines and synchronization overhead. We present Samyama, a high-performance graph-vector database written in Rust that unifies these workloads into a single engine. Samyama combines a RocksDB-backed persistent store with a versioned-arena MVCC model, a vectorized query executor with 35 physical operators, a cost-based query planner with plan enumeration and predicate pushdown, a dedicated CSR-based analytics engine, and native RDF/SPARQL support. The system integrates 22 metaheuristic optimization solvers directly into its query language, implements HNSW vector indexing with Graph RAG capabilities, and introduces Agentic Enrichment for autonomous graph expansion via LLMs. The Enterprise Edition adds GPU acceleration via wgpu, production-grade observability, point-in-time recovery, and hardened high availability with HTTP/2 Raft transport. Our evaluation on commodity hardware (Mac Mini M4, 16 GB RAM) demonstrates: ingestion at 255K nodes/s (CPU) and 412K nodes/s (GPU-accelerated); 115K Cypher queries/sec at 1M nodes; 4.0-4.7x latency reduction from late materialization on multi-hop traversals; 8.2x GPU PageRank speedup at 1M nodes; and 100% LDBC Graphalytics validation (28/28 tests). These results demonstrate that a unified graph-vector-optimization engine can achieve competitive performance on commodity hardware while maintaining Rust's memory safety guarantees.

核心结论
Samyama 统一图和向量数据库，通过库内联合优化消除跨系统数据移动，提升混合查询性能。
创新点
1. 统一图-向量数据库架构；2. 跨模态索引支持联合查询；3. 库内优化避免跨系统数据移动

08

GPU-RMQ: Accelerating Range Minimum Queries on Modern GPUs

arXiv 提交 2026-04-02 cs.DB cs.DC cs.DS 主题相关度 0.589

Lara Kreis, Justus Henneberg, Valentin Henkys, Felix Schuhknecht 等 5 人

中文摘要

本文提出了 GPU-RMQ，一种在现代 GPU 上加速范围最小查询（RMQ）的方法。RMQ 是许多算法和数据结构的基础操作，但传统 CPU 实现无法利用 GPU 的大规模并行性。GPU-RMQ 通过精心设计并行化策略和内存访问模式，在 GPU 上高效实现 RMQ，为依赖 RMQ 的数据库和算法应用提供加速基础。

查看英文原文摘要

Range minimum queries are frequently used in string processing and database applications including biological sequence analysis, document retrieval, and web search. Hence, various data structures have been proposed for improving their efficiency on both CPUs and GPUs.Recent work has also shown that hardware-accelerated ray tracing on modern NVIDIA RTX graphic cards can be exploited to answer range minimum queries by expressing queries as rays, which are fired into a scene of triangles representing minima of ranges at different granularities. While these approaches are promising, they suffer from at least one of three issues: severe memory overhead, high index construction time, and low query throughput. This renders these methods practically unusable on larger arrays: For example, the state-of-art GPU-based approaches LCA and RTXRMQ exceed the memory capacity of an NVIDIA RTX 4090 GPU for input arrays of size >= 2^29. To tackle these problems, in this work, we present a new approach called GPU-RMQ which is based on a hierarchical approach. GPU-RMQ first constructs a hierarchy of range minimum summaries on top of the original array in a highly parallel fashion. For query answering, only the relevant portions of the hierarchy are then processed in an optimized massively-parallel scan operation. Additionally, GPU-RMQ is hybrid in design enabling the use of both ray tracing cores and CUDA cores across different levels of the hierarchy to handle queries. Our experimental evaluation shows that GPU-RMQ outperforms the state-of-the-art approaches in terms of query throughput especially for larger arrays while offering a significantly lower memory footprint and up to two orders-of-magnitude faster index construction. In particular, it achieves up to ~8x higher throughput than LCA, ~17x higher throughput than RTXRMQ, and up to ~4800x higher throughput compared to an optimized CPU-based approach.

核心结论
GPU-RMQ 首次在现代 GPU 上高效实现范围最小查询，为依赖 RMQ 的数据库应用提供 GPU 加速基础。
创新点
1. 面向现代 GPU 的 RMQ 并行化策略；2. 优化内存访问模式提升 GPU 效率；3. 为数据库和算法应用提供 RMQ 加速原语

直接涉及 GPU 加速数据库操作

09

Scaling Worst-Case Optimal Datalog to GPUs

arXiv 提交 2026-04-22 cs.DB cs.PL 主题相关度 0.541

Yihao Sun, Kunting Qi, Thomas Gilray, Sidharth Kumar 等 5 人

中文摘要

GPU Datalog 引擎传统上基于二元连接，但对于 DOOP 和 ddisasm 等生产系统中出现的复杂多路查询，二元分解会导致 AGM 界的渐进式时空膨胀，引发 OOM 失败。最坏情况最优连接（WCOJ）可避免此膨胀，但其逐属性交集映射到 SIMT 硬件时在键偏斜下造成严重负载不均。本文提出 SRDatalog，首个基于 WCOJ 的 GPU Datalog 引擎，采用平面列式存储和两阶段确定性内存分配，并通过根级直方图引导负载均衡、结构化辅助关系分裂和流对齐规则多路复用缓解偏斜。在真实程序分析工作负载上实现 21x 到 47x 的几何平均加速。

查看英文原文摘要

Datalog is a declarative logic-programming language used for complex analytic reasoning workloads such as program analysis and graph analytics. Datalog's popularity is due to its unique price-point, marrying logic-defined specification with the potential for massive data parallelism. While traditional engines are CPU-based, the memory-bound nature of Datalog has led to increasing interest in leveraging GPUs. These engines beat CPU-based engines by operationalizing iterated relational joins via SIMT-friendly join algorithms. Unfortunately, all existing GPU Datalog engines are built on binary joins, which are inadequate for the complex multi-way queries arising in production systems such as DOOP and ddisasm. For these queries, binary decomposition can incur the AGM bound asymptotic blowup in time and space, leading to OOM failures regardless of join order. Worst-Case Optimal Joins (WCOJ) avoid this blowup, but their attribute-at-a-time intersections map poorly to SIMT hardware under key skew, causing severe load imbalance across Streaming Multiprocessors (SMs). We present SRDatalog, the first GPU Datalog engine based on WCOJ. SRDatalog uses flat columnar storage and two-phase deterministic memory allocation to avoid the OOM failures of binary joins and the index-rebuild overheads of static WCOJ systems. To mitigate skew and hide hardware stalls, SRDatalog further employs root-level histogram-guided load balancing, structural helper-relation splitting, and stream-aligned rule multiplexing. On real-world program-analysis workloads, SRDatalog achieves geometric-mean speedups of 21x to 47x.

核心结论
SRDatalog 是首个基于最坏情况最优连接的 GPU Datalog 引擎，在程序分析工作负载上实现 21-47 倍加速。
创新点
1. 首个基于 WCOJ 的 GPU Datalog 引擎；2. 平面列式存储+两阶段确定性内存分配避免 OOM；3. 直方图负载均衡+辅助关系分裂缓解键偏斜

直接涉及 GPU 加速数据库操作

10

ScaleGANN: Accelerate Large-Scale ANN Indexing by Cost-effective Cloud GPUs

arXiv 提交 2026-05-11 cs.DB 主题相关度 0.541

Lan Lu, Peiqi Yin, Isaac Yang, Tao Luo 等 8 人

中文摘要

基于图的 ANNS 算法因检索效率和准确性获得了广泛的研究兴趣和市场采用。现有方法主要依赖 CPU 进行图索引构建和检索，但对于大规模高维数据集耗时显著。GPU 方案虽然被探索，但 GPU 成本高且内存有限。本文提出 ScaleGANN，一种端到端系统，利用分布式云系统中低成本竞价 GPU 资源高效构建大规模高维数据集的图索引。ScaleGANN 采用分治合并思想，配合优化的向量分区算法提高索引时空效率，同时保证索引质量。其新颖的资源分配策略实现多 GPU 索引并行和构建查询的综合成本效益。实验表明，与最先进可扩展 ANNS 基准 DiskANN 相比，索引构建加速最多 9 倍，价格降低 6 倍。

查看英文原文摘要

Graph-based ANNS algorithms have gained increasing research interest and market adoption due to their efficiency and accuracy in retrieval. Existing approaches primarily rely on CPUs for graph index construction and retrieval, but this often requires significant time, especially for large-scale and high-dimensional datasets. Some studies have explored GPU-based solutions. However, GPUs are costly and their limited memory makes handling large datasets challenging. In this paper, we propose a novel end-to-end system ScaleGANN that enables users to efficiently construct graph indexes for large-scale, high-dimensional datasets by leveraging low-cost spot GPU resources in a distributed cloud system. ScaleGANN utilized the idea of divide-and-merge, with an optimized vector partitioning algorithm to further improve the indexing time and space efficiency while guaranteeing good index quality. Its novel resource allocation strategy realized multi-GPU indexing parallelism and overall cost-effectiveness for both build and query. Besides, we designed a task scheduler and cost model for better spot instance management and evaluation. We tested our system on large real-world datasets. Experiment results show that our approach can significantly accelerate the index build time to up to 9x times at even 6x lower price compared with the state-of-the-art extendable ANNS benchmark DiskANN.

核心结论
ScaleGANN 利用云竞价 GPU 实现大规模 ANNS 索引的分布式构建，在成本降低 6 倍的同时加速 9 倍。
创新点
1. 利用竞价 GPU 资源实现成本效益的分布式索引构建；2. 分治合并+向量分区优化索引时空效率；3. 任务调度和成本模型优化竞价实例管理

Towards Compute-Aware In-Switch Computing for LLMs Tensor-Parallelism on Multi-GPU Systems

arXiv 提交 2026-05-07 cs.AR cs.DC 主题相关度 0.520

Chen Zhang, Qijun Zhang, Zhuoshan Zhou, Yijia Diao 等 13 人

中文摘要

大规模 LLM 推理和训练中的张量并行（TP）引入了频繁的集合通信操作，主导了 GPU 间通信。虽然以 NVLink SHARP 为代表的交换机内计算通过减少冗余数据传输加速了集合操作，但其通信为中心的设计未考虑计算感知调度，限制了整体效率。本文提出计算感知的交换机内计算方法，优化多 GPU 系统上 LLM 张量并行的集合通信与计算重叠，提升端到端性能。

查看英文原文摘要

Tensor parallelism (TP) in large-scale LLM inference and training introduces frequent collective operations that dominate inter-GPU communication. While in-switch computing, exemplified by NVLink SHARP (NVLS), accelerates collective operations by reducing redundant data transfer, its communication-centric design philosophy introduces the mismatch between its communication mode and the memory semantic requirement of LLM's computation kernel. Such a mismatch isolates the compute and communication phases, resulting in underutilized resources and limited overlap in multi-GPU systems. To address the limitation, we propose CAIS, the first Compute-Aware In-Switch computing framework that aligns communication modes with computation's memory semantics requirement. CAIS consists of three integral techniques: (1) compute-aware ISA and microarchitecture extension to enable compute-aware in-switch computing. (2) merging-aware TB (Thread Block) coordination to improve the temporal alignment for efficient request merging. (3) graph-level dataflow optimizer to achieve a tight cross-kernel overlap. Evaluations on LLM workloads show that CAIS achieves 1.38$\times$ average end-to-end training speedup over the SOTA NVLS-enabled solution, and 1.61$\times$ over T3, the SOTA compute-communicate overlap solutions but do not leverage NVLS, demonstrating its effectiveness in accelerating TP on multi-GPU systems.

核心结论
提出计算感知的交换机内计算策略，优化 LLM 张量并行中集合通信与计算的重叠，突破 NVLink SHARP 通信为中心的设计局限。
创新点
1. 计算感知的交换机内计算调度策略；2. 优化集合通信与计算重叠；3. 面向多 GPU 张量并行的端到端性能提升

涉及 GPU 计算技术

Proxics: an efficient programming model for far memory accelerators

arXiv 提交 2026-04-20 cs.OS cs.AR cs.ET 主题相关度 0.513

Zikai Liu, Niels Pressel, Jasmin Schult, Roman Meier 等 6 人

中文摘要

CXL 内存池等分解式或远内存系统的使用重新激发了对近数据处理（NDP）的兴趣：将核心置于内存附近以减少与 CPU 之间的带宽需求。此类加速器的硬件设计正在出现，但缺乏干净、可移植的操作系统抽象。本文提出 Proxics，一种面向远内存加速器的高效编程模型，提供简洁可移植的 OS 抽象，使 NDP 加速器能高效访问远内存数据。

查看英文原文摘要

The use of disaggregated or far memory systems such as CXL memory pools has renewed interest in Near-Data Processing (NDP): situating cores close to memory to reduce bandwidth requirements to and from the CPU. Hardware designs for such accelerators are appearing, but there lack clean, portable OS abstractions for programming them. We propose a programming model for NDP devices based on familiar OS abstractions: virtual processors (processes) and inter-process communication channels (like Unix pipes). While appealing from a user perspective, a naive implementation of such abstractions is inappropriate for NDP accelerators: the paucity of processing power in some hardware designs makes classical processes overly heavyweight, and IPC based on shared buffers makes no sense in a system designed to reduce memory bandwidth. Accordingly, we show how to implement these abstractions in a lightweight and efficient manner by exploiting compilation and interconnect protocols. We demonstrate them with a real hardware platform runing applications with a range of memory access patterns, including bulk memory operations, in-memory databases and graph applications. Crucially, we show not only the benefits over CPU-only implementations, but also the critical importance of efficient, low-latency communication channels between CPU and NDP accelerators, a feature largely neglected in existing proposals.

核心结论
Proxics 为远内存加速器提供简洁可移植的编程模型和 OS 抽象，弥合 NDP 硬件与软件栈之间的鸿沟。
创新点
1. 面向远内存加速器的编程模型；2. 可移植的 OS 抽象简化 NDP 开发；3. 高效远内存数据访问机制

Toward a Universal GPU Instruction Set Architecture: A Cross-Vendor Analysis of Hardware-Invariant Computational Primitives in Parallel Processors

arXiv 提交 2026-03-22 cs.DC cs.AR 主题相关度 0.513

Ojima Abraham, Onyinye Okoli

中文摘要

本文首次对四大 GPU 厂商的指令集架构进行系统性跨厂商分析：NVIDIA（PTX ISA v1.0 至 v9.2，Fermi 至 Blackwell）、AMD（RDNA 1-4 和 CDNA 1-4）、Intel（Gen11、Xe-LP/HPG/HPC）和 Apple（G13，逆向工程）。通过识别硬件不变的计算原语，探索构建通用 GPU ISA 的可能性，为跨平台 GPU 编程和编译器设计提供基础。

查看英文原文摘要

We present the first systematic cross-vendor analysis of GPU instruction set architectures spanning all four major GPU vendors: NVIDIA (PTX ISA v1.0 through v9.2, Fermi through Blackwell), AMD (RDNA 1 to 4 and CDNA 1 to 4), Intel (Gen11, Xe-LP, Xe-HPG, Xe-HPC), and Apple (G13, reverse-engineered). Drawing on official ISA reference manuals, architecture whitepapers, patent filings, and community reverse-engineering efforts totaling over 5,000 pages of primary sources across 16 distinct microarchitectures, we identify ten hardware-invariant computational primitives that appear across all four architectures, six parameterizable dialects where vendors implement identical concepts with different parameters, and six true architectural divergences representing fundamental design disagreements. Based on this analysis, we propose an abstract execution model for a vendor-neutral GPU ISA grounded in the physical constraints of parallel computation. We validate our model with benchmark results on NVIDIA T4 and Apple M1 hardware, the two most architecturally distant platforms in our study. On five of six benchmark-platform pairs, the abstract model matches or exceeds native vendor-optimized performance. The single outlier (parallel reduction on NVIDIA, 62.5% of native) reveals that intra-wave shuffle must be a mandatory primitive, a finding that refines our proposed model.

核心结论
首次跨四大 GPU 厂商系统性分析 ISA，识别硬件不变计算原语，为通用 GPU ISA 和跨平台编译器奠定基础。
创新点
1. 首个覆盖四大 GPU 厂商的系统性 ISA 分析；2. 识别硬件不变计算原语；3. 为通用 GPU ISA 提供设计基础

Parallel R-tree-based Spatial Query Processing on a Commercial Processing-in-Memory System

arXiv 提交 2026-04-15 cs.DB cs.DC 主题相关度 0.511

Tasmia Jannat, Michael Gowanlock, Satish Puri

中文摘要

科学领域数据量的增长使空间查询处理因内存层次间高数据传输成本和有限内存带宽而日益困难。为解决这些瓶颈并减少数据移动能耗，本文探索在商用存内处理系统上实现基于 R 树的并行空间查询处理，将计算推至数据所在位置，大幅减少数据移动并提升空间查询性能。

查看英文原文摘要

The growing volume of data in scientific domains has made spatial query processing increasingly challenging due to high data transfer costs across the memory hierarchy and limited memory bandwidth. To address these bottlenecks and reduce the energy consumed on data movement, this work explores Processing-in-Memory (PIM) systems by executing range queries directly inside memory chips. Unlike prior PIM studies centered on linear scans or hash-based queries, this work is the first to map R-tree range queries onto commercial PIM hardware. The proposed broadcast-based method constructs the R-tree bottom-up on the CPU, broadcasts top levels to UPMEM DPUs (DRAM Processing Units) for global filtering, and distributes lower levels for parallel batched queries in a CPU-DPU system. We evaluate our approach on two real spatial datasets, Sports (999K rectangles) and Lakes (8.4M rectangles), and assess scalability using a synthetic dataset with up to 16M rectangles and 3.9M queries on a commercial UPMEM PIM system with up to 2,540 DPUs. Across all datasets, broadcast-based execution consistently outperforms subtree partitioning by preventing communication from dominating execution. On the Lakes dataset, strong scaling from 512 to 2,540 DPUs reduces kernel time from 64.9 s to 17.6 s, yielding up to 3.66x kernel and 2.70x end-to-end speedup relative to the CPU R-tree search on the same system. The PIM kernel also consumes approximately 3.4x less energy than the corresponding CPU search (e.g., 59.6 kJ vs. 167.0 kJ on Lakes), demonstrating scalable and energy-efficient hierarchical spatial range queries.

核心结论
在商用存内处理系统上实现并行 R 树空间查询，通过计算下推减少数据移动，突破内存带宽瓶颈。
创新点
1. 商用存内处理系统上的并行 R 树实现；2. 计算下推至数据位置减少传输；3. 解决空间查询的内存带宽瓶颈

Cuckoo-GPU: Accelerating Cuckoo Filters on Modern GPUs

arXiv 提交 2026-03-16 cs.DC cs.DB 主题相关度 0.507

Tim Dortmann, Markus Vieth, Bertil Schmidt

中文摘要

近似成员查询（AMQ）结构是数据库、网络和生物信息学等高吞吐量系统的基础组件。布隆过滤器虽然速度快但不支持删除操作。现有 GPU 动态替代方案（如 TCF 和 GPU Quotient Filter）在性能和功能上仍有不足。本文提出 Cuckoo-GPU，在现代 GPU 上加速布谷鸟过滤器，支持高效的插入、删除和查找操作，在高负载因子下仍保持高吞吐量。

查看英文原文摘要

Approximate Membership Query (AMQ) structures are essential for high-throughput systems in databases, networking, and bioinformatics. While Bloom filters offer speed, they lack support for deletions. Existing GPU-based dynamic alternatives, such as the Two-Choice Filter (TCF) and GPU Quotient Filter (GQF), enable deletions but incur severe performance penalties. We present Cuckoo-GPU, an open-source, high-performance Cuckoo filter library for GPUs. Instead of prioritizing cache locality, Cuckoo-GPU embraces the inherently random access pattern of Cuckoo hashing to fully saturate global memory bandwidth. Our design features a lock-free architecture built on atomic compare-and-swap operations, paired with a novel breadth-first search-based eviction heuristic that minimizes thread divergence and bounds sequential memory accesses during high-load insertions. Evaluated on NVIDIA GH200 (HBM3) and RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell (GDDR7) systems, Cuckoo-GPU closes the performance gap between append-only and dynamic AMQ structures. It achieves insertion, query, and deletion throughputs up to 378x (4.1x), 6x (34.7x), and 258x (107x) higher than GQF (TCF) on the same hardware, respectively, and delivers up to a 350x speedup over the fastest available multi-threaded CPU-based Cuckoo filter implementation. Moreover, its query throughput rivals that of the append-only GPU-based Blocked Bloom filter - demonstrating that dynamic AMQ structures can be deployed on modern accelerators without sacrificing performance.

核心结论
Cuckoo-GPU 在现代 GPU 上实现高效布谷鸟过滤器，支持动态操作且高负载因子下保持高吞吐量。
创新点
1. GPU 加速布谷鸟过滤器实现；2. 支持高效插入/删除/查找动态操作；3. 高负载因子下的稳定高吞吐量

中文摘要

大规模数据序列集合上的精确相似性搜索是现代应用的基础操作，但现有方案通常是碎片化、专用化或针对特定执行环境定制的。本文提出 DaiSy，一种统一的数据序列精确相似性搜索库，支持 CPU 和 GPU 执行环境，提供可扩展的索引和查询处理能力。

查看英文原文摘要

Exact similarity search over large collections of data series is a fundamental operation in modern applications, yet existing solutions are often fragmented, specialized, or tailored to specific execution environments. In this paper, we present DaiSy, a unified library for exact data series similarity search that integrates multiple state-of-the-art algorithms within a single, coherent framework. DaiSy is the first library to support exact similarity search across diverse execution environments, including implementations for disk-based, in-memory, GPU-accelerated, and distributed scalable similarity search. Although designed for data series, DaiSy is also directly applicable to exact similarity search over vector data, enabling its use in a broader range of applications. DaiSy is open-sourced and available at: https://github.com/MChatzakis/DaiSy.

Lyuheng Yuan, Da Yan, Akhlaque Ahmad, Fusheng Wang

中文摘要

空间连接是空间数据库的基本操作。随着 LiDAR 目标检测和 3D 数字病理学等应用中 3D 数据的快速增长，对 3D 数据集空间连接的需求日益增加。然而现有技术主要针对 2D 场景设计。本文提出 3DPipe，一种流水线式 GPU 框架，支持多面体对象上的可扩展广义空间连接，通过 GPU 并行化和流水线设计高效处理 3D 空间连接。

查看英文原文摘要

Spatial join is a fundamental operation in spatial databases. With the rapid growth of 3D data in applications such as LiDAR-based object detection and 3D digital pathology, there is an increasing need to support spatial join over 3D datasets. However, existing techniques are largely designed for 2D data, leaving 3D spatial join underexplored and computationally expensive. We present 3DPipe, a pipelined GPU framework for scalable spatial join over polyhedral objects. 3DPipe exploits GPU parallelism across both filtering and refinement stages, incorporates a multi-level pruning strategy for efficient candidate reduction, and employs chunked streaming to handle datasets exceeding GPU memory. Its pipelined execution overlaps CPU data preparation, host-device data transfer, and GPU computation to improve throughput. Experiments show that 3DPipe achieves up to 9.0$\times$ speedup over the state-of-the-art GPU solution, TDBase, while maintaining excellent scalability. 3DPipe is open-sourced at https://github.com/lyuheng/3dpipe.

核心结论
3DPipe 是首个面向 3D 多面体对象的 GPU 流水线空间连接框架，填补了 3D 空间数据库查询处理的空白。
创新点

GPU Acceleration of TFHE-Based High-Precision Nonlinear Layers for Encrypted LLM Inference

arXiv 提交 2026-04-06 cs.CR cs.AR 主题相关度 0.497

Guoci Chen, Xiurui Pan, Qiao Li, Bo Mao 等 8 人

中文摘要

将大语言模型部署为云服务引发隐私担忧，因为推理可能泄露敏感数据。全同态加密（FHE）允许对加密数据进行计算，但当前 FHE 方法在高效精确的非线性函数求值方面存在困难。本文提出 GPU 加速方案，针对基于 TFHE 的高精度非线性层进行优化，加速加密 LLM 推理中的非线性计算。

查看英文原文摘要

Deploying large language models (LLMs) as cloud services raises privacy concerns as inference may leak sensitive data. Fully Homomorphic Encryption (FHE) allows computation on encrypted data, but current FHE methods struggle with efficient and precise nonlinear function evaluation. Specifically, CKKS-based approaches require high-degree polynomial approximations, which are costly when target precision increases. Alternatively, TFHE's Programmable Bootstrapping (PBS) outperforms CKKS by offering exact lookup-table evaluation. But it lacks high-precision implementations of LLM nonlinear layers and underutilizes GPU resources. We propose \emph{TIGER}, the first GPU-accelerated framework for high-precision TFHE-based nonlinear LLM layer evaluation. TIGER offers: (1) GPU-optimized WoP-PBS method combined with numerical algorithms to surpass native lookup-table precision limits on nonlinear functions; (2) high-precision and efficient implementations of key nonlinear layers, enabling practical encrypted inference; (3) batch-driven design exploiting inter-input parallelism to boost GPU efficiency. TIGER achieves 7.17$\times$, 16.68$\times$, and 17.05$\times$ speedups over a CPU baseline for GELU, Softmax, and LayerNorm, respectively.

核心结论
GPU 加速基于 TFHE 的加密 LLM 推理中的高精度非线性层计算，推动隐私保护 LLM 推理走向实用。
创新点
1. GPU 加速 TFHE 高精度非线性层；2. 优化加密 LLM 推理的非线性计算；3. 推动隐私保护 LLM 推理实用化

Sim-FA: A GPGPU Simulator Framework for Fine-Grained FlashAttention Pipeline Analysis

arXiv 提交 2026-05-01 cs.AR 主题相关度 0.497

Zhongchun Zhou, Yuhang Gu, Chengtao Lai, Ya Wang 等 5 人

中文摘要

为高效支持大语言模型，现代 GPGPU 架构引入了 warp 特化等新特性和编程范式，实现生产者-消费者及矩阵乘法与激活函数的时间重叠。本文提出 Sim-FA，一种 GPGPU 模拟器框架，用于细粒度 FlashAttention 流水线分析，帮助研究者理解 warp 特化等新特性对 LLM 推理性能的影响。

查看英文原文摘要

To efficiently support Large Language Models (LLMs), modern GPGPU architectures have introduced new features and programming paradigms, such as warp specialization. These features enable temporal overlap between the producer and consumer, as well as between matrix multiplication and activation function operations, substantially improving performance. To conduct effective AI infrastructure and computer architecture research, cycle-accurate simulators that support these new features, together with analytical models that faithfully capture workload characteristics, are essential. However, existing academic tools provide limited support for these emerging requirements. Existing cycle-accurate simulators do not incorporate new NVIDIA GPU features, such as the Tensor Memory Accelerator (TMA), in a timely manner. Moreover, existing analytical models can misestimate DRAM traffic under certain configurations. In this paper, we build a simulation pipeline from FlashAttention-3 kernel instrumentation to cycle-accurate simulation. The simulator achieves a mean absolute percentage error (MAPE) of 5.7\% and a maximum absolute percentage error of 12.7\% against H800. We also provide a theoretical analysis of FlashAttention-3 and explain why existing analytical models can produce inaccurate traffic estimates.

核心结论
Sim-FA 提供细粒度 GPGPU 模拟框架，用于分析 FlashAttention 流水线和 warp 特化对 LLM 性能的影响。
创新点
1. 细粒度 GPGPU 模拟器框架；2. FlashAttention 流水线分析能力；3. 支持 warp 特化等新架构特性评估

GRAB-ANNS: High-Throughput Indexing and Hybrid Search via GPU-Native Bucketing

arXiv 提交 2026-03-31 cs.DB cs.IR 主题相关度 0.496

Xinkui Zhao, Hengxuan Lou, Yifan Zhang, Junjie Dai 等 6 人

中文摘要

混合搜索联合优化向量相似性和结构化谓词过滤，已成为现代 AI 驱动系统的基础构建块。虽然最近的谓词感知 ANN 索引提升了 CPU 上的过滤效率，但其性能越来越受限于有限内存带宽。本文提出 GRAB-ANNS，一种 GPU 原生分桶方案，通过 GPU 原生索引和混合搜索实现高吞吐量，突破 CPU 方案的内存带宽瓶颈。

查看英文原文摘要

Hybrid search, which jointly optimizes vector similarity and structured predicate filtering, has become a fundamental building block for modern AI-driven systems. While recent predicate-aware ANN indices improve filtering efficiency on CPUs, their performance is increasingly constrained by limited memory bandwidth and parallelism. Although GPUs offer massive parallelism and superior memory bandwidth, directly porting CPU-centric hybrid search algorithms to GPUs leads to severe performance degradation due to architectural mismatches, including irregular memory access, branch divergence, and excessive CPU-GPU synchronization. In this paper, we present GRAB-ANNS, a high-throughput, GPU-native graph index for dynamic hybrid search. Our key insight is to rethink hybrid indexing from a hardware-first perspective. We introduce a bucket-based memory layout that transforms range predicates into lightweight bucket selection, enabling coalesced memory accesses and efficient SIMT execution. To preserve global navigability under arbitrary filters, we design a hybrid graph topology that combines dense intra-bucket local edges with sparse inter-bucket remote edges. We further develop an append-only update pipeline that supports efficient batched insertions and parallel graph maintenance on GPUs. Extensive experiments on large-scale datasets show that GRAB-ANNS achieves up to 240.1 times higher query throughput and 12.6 times faster index construction than state-of-the-art CPU-based systems, and up to 10 times higher throughput compared to optimized GPU-native reimplementations, while maintaining high recall.

核心结论
GRAB-ANNS 通过 GPU 原生分桶实现高吞吐量混合搜索，突破 CPU 方案的内存带宽限制。
创新点
1. GPU 原生分桶索引方案；2. 高吞吐量向量+谓词混合搜索；3. 突破 CPU 内存带宽瓶颈

Evaluating CUDA Tile for AI Workloads on Hopper and Blackwell GPUs

arXiv 提交 2026-04-25 cs.LG cs.AI cs.AR 主题相关度 0.496

Divakar Kumar Yadav, Tian Zhao, Deepak Kumar

中文摘要

NVIDIA 的 CUDA Tile（CuTile）引入了基于 Python 的、以 Tile 为中心的抽象，旨在简化 GPU 核函数开发，同时保留 Tensor Core 和 Tensor Memory Accelerator（TMA）在现代 GPU 上的效率。本文首次对 CuTile 在 Hopper 和 Blackwell GPU 上的 AI 工作负载进行了独立跨架构评估，分析其编程便捷性和性能表现。

查看英文原文摘要

NVIDIA's CUDA Tile (CuTile) introduces a Python-based, tile-centric abstraction for GPU kernel development that aims to simplify programming while retaining Tensor Core and Tensor Memory Accelerator (TMA) efficiency on modern GPUs. We present the first independent, cross-architecture evaluation of CuTile against established approaches such as cuBLAS, Triton, WMMA, and raw SIMT on three NVIDIA GPUs spanning Hopper and Blackwell: H100 NVL, B200, and RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell Server Edition. We benchmark representative AI workloads, including GEMM, fused multi-head attention, and end-to-end LLM inference in BF16/FP16 precision, to assess both performance and portability. Our results show that CuTile effectiveness is strongly workload- and architecture-dependent. On datacenter-class Blackwell (B200), CuTile achieves up to 1007 TFLOP/s for fused attention, outperforming FlashAttention-2 by 2.5x while requiring only 60 lines of Python kernel code. For GEMM, CuTile reaches 52-79% of cuBLAS performance in 22 lines of code (versus 123 for WMMA), making it a practical replacement for hand-written CUDA kernels but not yet for vendor-optimized libraries. However, the same CuTile attention kernel achieves only 53% of FlashAttention-2 throughput on RTX PRO 6000 (sm_120), exposing significant cross-architecture optimization gaps. In contrast, Triton sustains 62-101% of cuBLAS performance across all tested platforms without architecture-specific tuning, demonstrating substantially stronger portability.

核心结论
首次独立评估 CUDA Tile 在 Hopper/Blackwell GPU 上的 AI 工作负载表现，为开发者提供编程抽象与性能的权衡参考。
创新点
1. 首次独立跨架构评估 CuTile；2. 分析编程便捷性与性能的权衡；3. 覆盖 Hopper 和 Blackwell 两代 GPU

GEMM-GS: Accelerating 3D Gaussian Splatting on Tensor Cores with GEMM-Compatible Blending

arXiv 提交 2026-04-02 cs.AR cs.GR 主题相关度 0.491

Haomin Li, Bowen Zhu, Fangxin Liu, Zongwu Wang 等 7 人

中文摘要

神经辐射场（NeRF）能从多张 2D 图像重建 3D 场景，但点采样设计导致高渲染延迟。3D 高斯溅射（3DGS）通过显式场景表示和优化流水线改进了 NeRF，但仍无法满足实时需求。本文提出 GEMM-GS，通过 GEMM 兼容的混合将 3DGS 加速到 Tensor Core 上，利用张量计算单元实现实时 3D 渲染。

查看英文原文摘要

Neural Radiance Fields (NeRF) enables 3D scene reconstruction from several 2D images but incurs high rendering latency via its point-sampling design. 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) improves on NeRF with explicit scene representation and an optimized pipeline yet still fails to meet practical real-time demands. Existing acceleration works overlook the evolving Tensor Cores of modern GPUs because 3DGS pipeline lacks General Matrix Multiplication (GEMM) operations. This paper proposes GEMM-GS, an acceleration approach utilizing tensor cores on GPUs via GEMM-friendly blending transformation. It equivalently reformulates the 3DGS blending process into a GEMM-compatible form to utilize Tensor Cores. A high-performance CUDA kernel is designed, integrating a three-stage double-buffered pipeline that overlaps computation and memory access. Extensive experiments show that GEMM-GS achieves $1.42\times$ speedup over vanilla 3DGS and provides an additional $1.47\times$ speedup on average when combining with existing acceleration approaches. Code is released at https://github.com/shieldforever/GEMM-GS.

核心结论
GEMM-GS 将 3D 高斯溅射的混合操作转换为 GEMM 兼容格式，利用 Tensor Core 实现实时 3D 渲染。
创新点
1. GEMM 兼容混合策略适配 Tensor Core；2. 将 3DGS 渲染操作映射到矩阵乘法；3. 实现实时 3D 场景渲染

涉及 GPU 计算技术

A comprehensive evaluation of spatial co-execution on GPUs using MPS and MIG technologies

arXiv 提交 2026-04-24 cs.DC cs.AR 主题相关度 0.488

Jorge Villarrubia, Luis Costero, Francisco D. Igual, Katzalin Olcoz

中文摘要

为缓解现代 GPU 日益普遍的计算资源低利用率问题，空间共享方法允许多个应用同时使用 GPU。本文对 NVIDIA 实现该目标的两大技术——多进程服务（MPS）和多实例 GPU（MIG）——进行全面评估，分析不同工作负载下的空间共执行性能、隔离性和资源利用效率。

查看英文原文摘要

To mitigate the increasingly common underutilization of computational resources in modern GPUs, spatial sharing methods enable multiple applications to use them simultaneously. This work presents a comprehensive evaluation of NVIDIA's primary technologies to achieve that goal: Multi-Process Service (MPS) and Multi-Instance GPU (MIG). Our findings reveal a crucial trade-off between MPS's flexibility and MIG's isolation, and provide many key insights for improving the co-execution strategy according to job profiles. In the most favorable scenarios, MPS improves performance by up to 30% and reduces energy by about 20%, using its provisioning option to avoid resource monopolization. However, under memory contention, it suffers severe degradation, worsening performance by around 30%. Conversely, MIG's full hardware isolation resolves memory contention, leading to more consistent improvements, but these gains are tempered by higher overhead, and its rigid scheme can degrade performance in certain cases.

核心结论
全面评估 NVIDIA MPS 和 MIG 两种 GPU 空间共享技术，为多应用 GPU 共执行的配置选择提供实证参考。
创新点
1. 对 MPS 和 MIG 的全面评估；2. 分析不同工作负载下的共执行性能；3. 评估隔离性和资源利用效率

Characterizing CPU-Induced Slowdowns in Multi-GPU LLM Inference

arXiv 提交 2026-03-24 cs.AR cs.DC 主题相关度 0.483

Euijun Chung, Yuxiao Jia, Aaron Jezghani, Hyesoon Kim

中文摘要

大规模机器学习工作负载越来越依赖多 GPU 系统，但其性能常被一个被忽视的组件限制：CPU。通过对现代 LLM 推理和服务工作负载的详细研究，发现多 GPU 性能常因 CPU 引起的减速而退化。本文系统刻画了 CPU 导致多 GPU LLM 推理减速的根本原因和影响程度，为系统优化提供指导。

查看英文原文摘要

Large-scale machine learning workloads increasingly rely on multi-GPU systems, yet their performance is often limited by an overlooked component: the CPU. Through a detailed study of modern large language model (LLM) inference and serving workloads, we find that multi-GPU performance frequently degrades not because GPUs are saturated, but because CPUs fail to keep the GPUs busy. Under limited CPU allocations, systems exhibit symptoms such as delayed kernel launch, stalled communication, and increased tokenization latency, leading to severe GPU underutilization even when ample GPU resources are available. These bottlenecks persist even in serving stacks that employ process-level separation and modern GPU-side optimizations such as CUDA Graphs. Since the marginal cost of additional CPU cores is small relative to GPU instance pricing, our evaluation indicates that increasing the number of CPU cores can substantially improve performance and stability at minimal additional cost. Under moderate serving load, we observe that CPU-starved configurations frequently time out, while providing adequate CPU resources restores responsiveness and reduces time-to-first-token (TTFT) latency by 1.47--5.15x across configurations, all without requiring additional GPUs.

核心结论
揭示 CPU 是多 GPU LLM 推理中被忽视的性能瓶颈，系统刻画了 CPU 导致减速的根因和影响。
创新点
1. 首次系统刻画 CPU 对多 GPU LLM 推理的减速影响；2. 识别 CPU 瓶颈的根本原因；3. 为多 GPU 系统优化提供指导

CuLifter: Lifting GPU Binaries to Typed IR

arXiv 提交 2026-04-30 cs.AR 主题相关度 0.472

Jisheng Zhao, Huanzhi Pu, Shinnung Jeong, Chihyo Ahn 等 5 人

中文摘要

GPU 编译器将所有数据类型合并到统一寄存器文件中，抹去了二进制分析工具依赖的类型信息。本文证明从此无类型寄存器文件中恢复类型是 GPU 二进制提升的核心挑战。提出 CuLifter，一种 SASS 到 LLVM IR 的提升框架，通过类型推断和恢复实现 GPU 二进制到类型化 IR 的转换。

查看英文原文摘要

GPU compilers merge all data types into a single unified register file, erasing the type information that binary-analysis tools rely on. We show that type recovery from this untyped register file is the central challenge of GPU binary lifting. We present CuLifter, a SASS-to-LLVM IR lifting framework that recovers register types via constraint propagation with conflict detection, reconstructs explicit control flow, and aggregates multi-instruction patterns. Across eight benchmark suites (24,437 GPU functions in 919 cubins) spanning open-source applications, vendor libraries, and optimized ML runtimes, CuLifter successfully lifts 99.98% of functions to valid LLVM IR. An ablation study confirms that type recovery is the only step required to produce semantically correct IR: disabling it drops the x86 pass rate from 73.8% to 0%, a 73.8 percentage-point drop.

核心结论
CuLifter 解决 GPU 二进制提升中类型信息丢失的核心挑战，实现从 SASS 到类型化 LLVM IR 的转换。
创新点
1. 识别 GPU 二进制提升的类型恢复为核心挑战；2. SASS 到 LLVM IR 的类型推断框架；3. 支持类型化 GPU 二进制分析

Fleet: Hierarchical Task-based Abstraction for Megakernels on Multi-Die GPUs

arXiv 提交 2026-04-15 cs.AR 主题相关度 0.470

Sangeeta Chowdhary, Ryan Swann, Sean Siddens, Muhammad Osama 等 10 人

中文摘要

现代 GPU 采用基于小芯片的设计，具有多个私有缓存层次，但当前编程模型（CUDA/HIP）暴露的是扁平执行层次，无法表达小芯片级的局部性或同步。这种不匹配导致内存受限工作负载（如 LLM 推理）中的冗余内存流量和低缓存利用率。本文提出 Fleet，一种多级任务模型，将计算任务映射到小芯片拓扑，优化多芯片 GPU 上的数据局部性和缓存利用。

查看英文原文摘要

Modern GPUs adopt chiplet-based designs with multiple private cache hierarchies, but current programming models (CUDA/HIP) expose a flat execution hierarchy that cannot express chiplet-level locality or synchronization. This mismatch leads to redundant memory traffic and poor cache utilization in memory-bound workloads such as LLM inference. We present Fleet, a multi-level task model that maps computation to memory scopes. Fleet introduces Chiplet-tasks, a new abstraction that binds work and data to a chiplet and enables coordination through its shared L2 cache. Wavefront-level, CU-level, and device-level tasks align with existing abstractions, while Chiplet-tasks expose a previously unaddressed level of the hierarchy. Fleet is implemented as a persistent kernel runtime with per-chiplet scheduling, allowing workers within a chiplet to cooperatively execute tasks with coordinated cache reuse. On AMD Instinct MI350 with Qwen3-8B, Fleet achieves 1.3-1.5x lower decode latency than vLLM at batch sizes 1-8 through persistent kernel execution and per-chiplet scheduling. At larger batch sizes, cooperative weight tiling increases L2 hit rate (from 12% to 54% at batch size 32 and from 39% to 61% at batch size 64), reducing HBM traffic by up to 37% and delivering 1.27-1.30x speedup over a chiplet-unaware megakernel baseline.

核心结论
Fleet 提出多级任务抽象解决 chiplet GPU 的编程模型不匹配问题，通过小芯片感知的任务调度优化数据局部性。
创新点
1. 多级任务模型映射 chiplet 拓扑；2. 小芯片感知的数据局部性优化；3. 解决扁平编程模型与层次化硬件的不匹配

涉及 GPU 计算技术

Optimizing Relational Queries over Array-Valued Data in Columnar Systems

arXiv 提交 2026-04-02 cs.DB 主题相关度 0.460

Maroua Zeblah, Etienne Couritas, Sarah Chlyah, Pierre Genevès 等 6 人

中文摘要

现代分析工作负载越来越多地结合关系数据和数组值属性。虽然列式数据库系统能高效处理此类工作负载，但优化交替执行关系算子和数组操作的能力仍然有限。本文引入 A3D-RA，一种支持数组值属性的扩展关系代数，以及全面的查询优化框架，使列式系统能高效处理关系与数组混合查询。

查看英文原文摘要

Modern analytical workloads increasingly combine relational data with array-valued attributes. While columnar database systems efficiently process such workloads, their ability to optimize queries that interleave relational operators with array manipulations remains limited. This paper introduces A3D-RA, an extended relational algebra supporting array-valued attributes, together with a comprehensive framework for algebraic reasoning and optimization. We formalize its data model and semantics, develop a complete set of equivalence-preserving transformation rules capturing pairwise interactions between relational and array operators, and propose a plan enumeration strategy with an optimality guarantee that remains polynomial in all non-join operators. We design A3D-RA as a modular, backend-independent optimization layer that can be instantiated over existing analytical database systems. Experimental results across three high-performance engines on a real-world workload show consistent performance gains enabled by the proposed algebraic optimization layer.

核心结论
A3D-RA 扩展关系代数支持数组值属性，使列式数据库能高效优化关系与数组混合查询。
创新点
1. 扩展关系代数支持数组值属性（A3D-RA）；2. 关系与数组混合查询优化框架；3. 列式系统中的数组操作高效执行

与数据库系统和查询处理相关

MoE-Hub: Taming Software Complexity for Seamless MoE Overlap with Hardware-Accelerated Communication on Multi-GPU Systems

arXiv 提交 2026-05-07 cs.AR cs.DC 主题相关度 0.457

Zhuoshan Zhou, Chen Zhang, Shuyi Zhang, Qijun Zhang 等 13 人

中文摘要

混合专家（MoE）架构对扩展大语言模型至关重要，但其可扩展性严重受限于多 GPU 系统中的 GPU 间通信瓶颈。虽然通信与计算重叠是广泛认可的优化，但其有效部署在性能和可编程性方面仍面临挑战。本文提出 MoE-Hub，通过硬件加速通信和软件复杂度管理，实现 MoE 在多 GPU 系统上的无缝通信计算重叠。

查看英文原文摘要

The Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architecture is crucial for scaling large language models, but its scalability is severely limited by inter-GPU communication bottlenecks in multi-GPU systems. Although overlapping communication with computation is a widely recognized optimization, its effective deployment still remains challenging, both in terms of performance and programmability. In this work, we identify the root cause as a fundamental abstraction mismatch between MoE's dynamic, irregular token-to-expert mapping and the static, address-centric communication model of modern GPUs, which necessitates a complex software mediation phase to resolve addresses before data transfers, limiting performance and software flexibility. To resolve this, we propose MoE-Hub, a hardware-software co-design that introduces a destination-agnostic communication paradigm. MoE-Hub decouples data transmission from address management, allowing producers to send data immediately after routing using only a logical destination, while address allocation and data-flow orchestration are handled transparently by lightweight hardware in the GPU hub. By hardware-accelerating the entire communication control plane, MoE-Hub enables seamless and transparent overlap. Our evaluation shows that MoE-Hub achieves 1.40x-3.08x per-layer and 1.21x-1.98x end-to-end speedup over state-of-the-art systems.

核心结论
MoE-Hub 解决 MoE 多 GPU 通信与计算重叠的软件复杂度问题，实现硬件加速通信下的无缝重叠。
创新点
1. 硬件加速通信与计算的无缝重叠；2. MoE 通信计算重叠的软件复杂度管理；3. 多 GPU 系统上的 MoE 可扩展性提升

涉及 GPU 计算技术

gMatch: Fine-Grained and Hardware-Efficient Subgraph Matching on GPUs

arXiv 提交 2026-04-12 cs.DB 主题相关度 0.456

Weitian Chen, Shixuan Sun, Cheng Chen, Yongmin Hu 等 6 人

中文摘要

子图匹配是图分析的核心操作，支持从社交网络分析到生物信息学的广泛应用。现有 GPU 方案通过并行化加速子图匹配，但依赖粗粒度执行模型，因高内存开销和线程低利用率而面临可扩展性和效率问题。本文提出 gMatch，一种细粒度且硬件高效的 GPU 子图匹配方法，通过精细的任务划分和硬件感知优化提升可扩展性。

查看英文原文摘要

Subgraph matching is a core operation in graph analytics, supporting a broad spectrum of applications from social network analysis to bioinformatics. Recent GPU-based approaches accelerate subgraph matching by leveraging parallelism but rely on a coarse-grained execution model that suffers from scalability and efficiency issues due to high memory overhead and thread underutilization. In this paper, we propose gMatch, a hardware-efficient subgraph matching approach on GPUs. gMatch introduces a fine-grained execution model that reduces memory consumption and enables flexible task scheduling among threads. We further design warp-level batch exploration and lightweight load balancing to improve execution efficiency and scalability. Experiments on diverse workloads and real-world datasets show that gMatch outperforms state-of-the-art subgraph matching methods, including STMatch, T-DFS, and EGSM, in both performance and scalability. We also compare against state-of-the-art systems for mining small patterns, such as BEEP and G$^2$Miner. While these systems achieve better performance on small datasets, gMatch scales to substantially larger queries and datasets, where existing approaches degrade or fail to complete.

核心结论
gMatch 通过细粒度执行模型和硬件感知优化突破 GPU 子图匹配的可扩展性和效率瓶颈。
创新点
1. 细粒度子图匹配执行模型；2. 硬件感知的 GPU 优化策略；3. 解决粗粒度方案的内存开销和线程利用率问题

AME-PIM: Can Memory be Your Next Tensor Accelerator?

arXiv 提交 2026-04-30 cs.AR 主题相关度 0.450

Emanuele Venieri, Simone Manoni, Alberto Florian, Jaehyun Park 等 6 人

中文摘要

高带宽存内处理内存（HBM-PIM）提供了通过在内存内直接执行计算来减少数据移动的机会，但当前商业平台暴露的指令集有限且需要专用软件栈。本文研究 HBM-PIM 是否可作为 ISA 级矩阵加速的后端，使用 RISC-V 附加矩阵扩展探索将内存作为下一代张量加速器的可能性。

查看英文原文摘要

High Bandwidth Memory with Processing-in-Memory (HBM-PIM) offers an opportunity to reduce data movement by executing computation directly inside memory, but current commercial platforms expose limited instruction sets and require specialized software stacks. In this work, we investigate whether HBM-PIM can serve as a backend for ISA-level matrix acceleration, using the RISC-V Attached Matrix Extension (AME) as a semantic reference. We propose a PEP-based execution model that maps AME element-wise and matrix instructions to HBM-PIM micro-kernels and data instructions in memory operations. Differently from SoA HBM-PIM, we introduce a reduction-free outer-product dataflow that enables accumulation entirely within memory despite the lack of native reduction support. Our approach supports end-to-end execution of element-wise operations, GEMV, and GEMM in PIM mode, minimizing host involvement and off-chip transfers. An experimental evaluation on Samsung Aquabolt-XL shows that AME matrix tile multiplication achieves up to 14.9 GFLOP/s (59.4 FLOP/cycle) on a single HBM pseudo-channel.

核心结论
探索 HBM-PIM 作为 ISA 级矩阵加速后端的可行性，提出内存即张量加速器的新范式。
创新点
1. 研究 HBM-PIM 作为矩阵加速后端；2. 基于 RISC-V 矩阵扩展的 ISA 级加速；3. 内存即张量加速器的新架构范式

与硬件架构或数据系统相关

The xPU-athalon: Quantifying the Competition of AI Acceleration

arXiv 提交 2026-04-12 cs.AR 主题相关度 0.449

Alicia Golden, Carole-Jean Wu, Gu-Yeon Wei, David Brooks

中文摘要

对更高 AI 计算效率的追求催生了多种加速器架构，它们日益挑战 GPU 长期以来的主导地位。本文对不断演变的 AI 加速器格局进行量化分析，涵盖 Cerebras CS-3、SambaNova SN-40、Groq、Gaudi 和 TPUv5e 平台，并与 NVIDIA（A100、H100）和 AMD（MI-300X）GPU 对比。我们分别通过（1）端到端工作负载和（2）单项计算原语的基准测试，评估延迟、吞吐量、功耗和能效之间的关键权衡。我们发现，最优硬件平台随批大小、序列长度和模型规模而变化，表明存在广阔的优化空间。我们详细测量了大语言模型（LLM）推理的预填充和解码阶段的功耗，并量化了通信的能耗。我们还发现，Cerebras、SambaNova 和 Gaudi 的空闲功耗比 NVIDIA 和 AMD GPU 高 10%–60%，表明要实现其宣称的能效收益，维持较高的利用率十分重要。最后，我们根据对真实工作负载开展的性能剖析实验评估各平台的可编程性，比较编译时间以及达到所宣称性能所需的软件栈成熟度。

查看英文原文摘要

The push for greater efficiency in AI computation has given rise to an array of accelerator architectures that increasingly challenge the GPU's long-standing dominance. In this work, we provide a quantitative view of this evolving landscape of AI accelerators, including the Cerebras CS-3, SambaNova SN-40, Groq, Gaudi, and TPUv5e platforms, and compare against both NVIDIA (A100, H100) and AMD (MI-300X) GPUs. We evaluate key trade-offs in latency, throughput, power consumption, and energy-efficiency across both (i) end-to-end workloads and (ii) benchmarks of individual computational primitives. Notably, we find the optimal hardware platform varies across batch size, sequence length, and model size, revealing a large underlying optimization space. Our analysis includes detailed power measurements across the prefill and decode phases of LLM inference, as well as quantification of the energy cost of communication. We additionally find that Cerebras, SambaNova, and Gaudi have 10-60% higher idle power than NVIDIA and AMD GPUs, emphasizing the importance of high utilization in order to realize promised efficiency gains. Finally, we assess programmability across platforms based on our experiments with real profiled workloads, comparing the compilation times and software stack maturity required to achieve promised performance.

核心结论
最优 AI 加速平台随批大小、序列长度和模型规模而变化。Cerebras、SambaNova 和 Gaudi 的空闲功耗比受测的 NVIDIA 和 AMD GPU 高 10%–60%，因此维持高利用率对于实现其宣称的能效收益尤为重要。
创新点
1. 结合端到端工作负载与单项计算原语基准，量化比较多种 AI 加速器与 NVIDIA、AMD GPU 的延迟、吞吐量、功耗和能效；2. 分阶段测量 LLM 推理的预填充和解码功耗，并量化通信能耗；3. 基于真实工作负载的性能剖析，比较各平台的编译时间和达到所宣称性能所需的软件栈成熟度。

涉及 GPU 计算技术

DICE: Enabling Efficient General-Purpose SIMT Execution with Statically Scheduled Coarse-Grained Reconfigurable Arrays

arXiv 提交 2026-05-06 cs.AR 主题相关度 0.449

Jiayi Wang, Ang Da Lu, Zhichen Zeng, Ang Li

中文摘要

GPU 凭借单指令多线程（SIMT）编程模型主导了大规模并行计算，但其底层单指令多数据（SIMD）执行方式因频繁访问寄存器文件（RF）和需要复杂的控制逻辑而带来显著的能耗开销。本文提出 DICE 架构，使用开销极低、采用静态调度的粗粒度可重构阵列（CGRA）替换 SIMD 后端，以解决这些低效问题。传统 SIMD 单元以锁步方式执行线程束，DICE 则将活跃线程以流水方式派发到 CGRA 阵列上，使数据在处理单元（PE）之间直接传递，减少因中间值而产生的寄存器文件访问。为在保留静态调度的同时处理具有运行时动态性的操作，例如延迟可变的内存加载和依赖数据的控制流，DICE 沿动态依赖边对程序进行划分，将划分后的部分映射到不同的 CGRA 配置，从而将程序编译为“p-graph”。DICE 还引入了几项关键优化，包括通过双缓冲配置存储器隐藏重配置延迟、在编译时展开 p-graph 以提高资源利用率，以及通过时序内存请求合并单元（TMCU）合并流水线中相邻线程的内存请求。在 Accel-Sim 中使用 Rodinia 基准套件进行的评估表明，DICE 平均减少了 68% 的寄存器文件访问。在计算和存储资源等同的条件下，相比建模得到的 NVIDIA Turing 流式多处理器（SM），DICE 的 CGRA 处理器（CP）的动态能效为其 1.77–1.90 倍（几何平均值），平均功耗降低 42.0%–45.9%。完整 DICE 系统的性能与建模得到的 Turing GPU 基线相当。DICE 表明，空间流水执行能够在保持性能的同时显著降低能耗。

查看英文原文摘要

While GPUs dominate massively parallel computing through the single-instruction, multiple-thread (SIMT) programming model, their underlying single-instruction, multiple-data (SIMD) execution incurs substantial energy overhead from frequent register file (RF) accesses and complex control logic. We present DICE, a novel architecture that addresses these inefficiencies by replacing the SIMD backend with minimal-overhead, statically scheduled coarse-grained reconfigurable arrays (CGRAs). Unlike SIMD units that execute warps of threads in lockstep, DICE dispatches active threads in a pipelined manner onto the CGRA fabric, where data flow directly between processing elements (PEs), reducing RF accesses for intermediate values. To handle operations with runtime dynamism, such as variable-latency memory loads and data-dependent control flow, while preserving static scheduling, DICE compiles programs into "p-graphs" by partitioning dynamic dependence edges across separate CGRA configurations. DICE further introduces several key optimizations: double-buffered configuration memory to hide reconfiguration latency, compile-time p-graph unrolling to enhance resource utilization, and a temporal memory coalescing unit (TMCU) to merge memory requests from consecutive, pipelined threads. Evaluations on Rodinia benchmarks in Accel-sim demonstrate that DICE reduces register file accesses by 68% on average. With equivalent computation and memory resources, DICE's CGRA Processors (CPs) achieve a geometric mean of 1.77-1.90x dynamic energy efficiency and 42.0%-45.9% average power reduction compared to the modeled NVIDIA Turing Streaming Multiprocessors (SMs), while the full DICE system achieves performance comparable to the modeled Turing GPU baselines. DICE demonstrates that spatial pipeline execution can deliver substantial energy savings without sacrificing performance.

核心结论
Accel-Sim 上的 Rodinia 评估显示，DICE 平均减少了 68% 的寄存器文件访问。在计算和存储资源等同的条件下，其 CGRA 处理器的动态能效为建模的 NVIDIA Turing SM 的 1.77–1.90 倍（几何平均值），平均功耗降低 42.0%–45.9%，完整系统性能与建模的 Turing GPU 基线相当。
创新点
1. 以静态调度的 CGRA 替换 SIMD 后端，流水派发活跃线程，并在处理单元之间直接传递中间值；2. 沿动态依赖边划分程序并编译为 p-graph，在静态调度下支持运行时动态操作；3. 采用双缓冲配置存储器隐藏重配置延迟；4. 在编译时展开 p-graph 以提高资源利用率；5. 利用 TMCU 合并流水线中相邻线程的内存请求。

Microbenchmark-Driven Analytical Performance Modeling Across Modern GPU Architectures

arXiv 提交 2026-05-05 cs.DC cs.AR 主题相关度 0.449

Aaron Jarmusch, Sunita Chandrasekaran

中文摘要

GPU 架构快速演进，采用了复杂的存储层次结构、矩阵运算单元和多种精度格式，理论峰值与实际可达性能之间的差距持续扩大。我们以系统性的微基准测试和特征刻画为基础，为 NVIDIA Blackwell（B200）和 AMD CDNA3（MI300A）设计并构建了解析性能模型。Blackwell 模型涵盖张量存储器（TMEM）、异步批量复制（TMA）和第 5 代张量核心。CDNA3 模型涵盖 Infinity Cache 层次结构、向量通用寄存器（VGPR）约束和占用率。验证结果显示，B200 上 21 个内核的平均绝对误差（MAE）为 1.31%，MI300A 上 27 个内核的 MAE 为 0.09%，而朴素 Roofline 基线在相同内核上的误差超过 95%。我们还使用 Rodinia 3.1 和 SPEChpc 2021 Tiny 对模型进行了进一步验证。我们更新了模型中的高带宽内存（HBM）带宽、容量及缓存参数，并将模型应用于 H200（Hopper）和 MI250X（CDNA2）。结果表明，模型结构无需大幅调整。论文录用后，所有模型和基准测试都将开源发布。

查看英文原文摘要

Rapidly evolving GPU architectures featuring complex memory hierarchies, matrix units, and varied precision formats continue to widen the gap between theoretical peaks and achievable performance. We design and develop analytical performance models for NVIDIA Blackwell (B200) and AMD CDNA3 (MI300A) grounded in systematic microbenchmark characterization. For Blackwell, the model captures Tensor Memory (TMEM), asynchronous bulk copy (TMA), and 5th-generation tensor cores; for CDNA3, the model captures Infinity Cache hierarchy, VGPR constraints, and occupancy. Validation yields 1.31% MAE on B200 (21 kernels) and 0.09% on MI300A (27 kernels), while naive roofline baselines exceed 95% error on the same kernels. We further validate the models using Rodinia~3.1 and SPEChpc 2021 Tiny.The models are updated with HBM bandwidth, capacity, and cache parameters and applied to H200 (Hopper) and MI250X (CDNA2), indicating no major restructuring of the models are needed. All models and benchmarks will be released as open-source upon acceptance.

核心结论
在 B200 的 21 个内核和 MI300A 的 27 个内核上，解析模型的 MAE 分别为 1.31% 和 0.09%，朴素 Roofline 基线在相同内核上的误差超过 95%。更新 HBM 带宽、容量及缓存参数后，模型可应用于 H200 和 MI250X，无需大幅调整结构。
创新点
1. 基于系统性微基准测试构建 B200 和 MI300A 的解析性能模型，分别纳入 TMEM、TMA 和第 5 代张量核心，以及 Infinity Cache 层次结构、VGPR 约束和占用率；2. 通过更新 HBM 带宽、容量及缓存参数，将模型应用于 H200 和 MI250X。

FPGA-Accelerated Lock Management and Transaction Processing: Architecture, Optimization, and Design Space Exploration

arXiv 提交 2026-05-13 cs.AR cs.DB cs.DC 主题相关度 0.448

Shien Zhu, Gustavo Alonso

中文摘要

联机事务处理（OLTP）是一类经典应用，相关业务仍在增长。基于 CPU 的 OLTP 在处理锁请求时效率较低，主要原因是大多数锁很少被访问，锁代理必须频繁访问内存，读取锁的详细信息以判断是否授予锁。为此，我们提出集成锁表的专用硬件锁代理，以消除 DRAM 访问开销。 本文提出面向数据库系统的硬件加速锁管理和事务处理方案。首先，我们提出一种低延迟锁代理，同时针对加锁和解锁请求进行优化。其次，我们设计了一种可扩展的事务代理，负责执行事务的完整生命周期。我们介绍了所提锁管理与事务处理系统的架构、优化方法和设计空间探索。实验结果表明，在 TPC-C 基准测试中，事务吞吐量最高达到 CPU 基线的 51 倍。

查看英文原文摘要

Online Transaction Processing (OLTP) is a classic application with a growing business. CPU-based OLTP has low lock serving efficiency. The main reason is that most locks are cold, and the lock agent must issue frequent memory accesses to retrieve the lock details to determine whether to grant it. This motivates us to propose dedicated hardware-based lock agents with integrated lock tables to remove the DRAM access overhead. In this paper, we propose hardware-accelerated lock management and transaction processing for database systems. First, we propose a low-latency lock agent optimized for both lock acquiring and releasing requests. Second, we design a scalable transaction agent that executes the full transaction lifecycle. We present the architecture, optimizations, and design-space exploration of the proposed lock management and transaction processing system. The experiment results show up to 51X higher transaction throughput over the CPU baseline on the TPC-C benchmark.

核心结论
该方案通过在专用硬件锁代理中集成锁表来消除 DRAM 访问开销，并由可扩展的事务代理处理完整事务生命周期。在 TPC-C 基准测试中，事务吞吐量最高达到 CPU 基线的 51 倍。
创新点
1. 设计集成锁表的低延迟专用硬件锁代理，同时优化加锁和解锁请求；2. 设计执行完整事务生命周期的可扩展事务代理；3. 对所提锁管理与事务处理系统进行架构设计、优化和设计空间探索。

CS-PQ: Cache-Friendly SIMD Product Quantization for Large-Scale ANNS Index Construction

arXiv 提交 2026-05-25 cs.DB 主题相关度 0.448

Y. T. Ma, K. C. Huang, X. K. Jiang, M. L. Wang 等 8 人

中文摘要

乘积量化（PQ）构建是近似最近邻搜索（ANNS）向量索引构建的重要环节。向量维度和数量的快速增长显著增加了 PQ 的计算成本。PQ 构建采用“一对一”执行模式，即每次计算对应一次数据加载，数据传输开销占主导，因此现有的 GPU 加速方案不适合 PQ 构建。基于 CPU 的方案虽已广泛使用，但大多采用通用设计，未能利用 PQ 构建的固有特性。本文提出 CS-PQ，这是一个面向现代 CPU、具备缓存友好性并采用 SIMD 优化的 PQ 框架。CS-PQ 引入面向向量的 SIMD 执行方式，沿 PQ 质心这一轴进行向量化，以替代沿子向量维度的向量化，从而将量化粒度与 SIMD 宽度解耦。它进一步重组执行流水线以改善缓存局部性，并改写 PQ 计算，在保持正确性的同时消除冗余操作。大规模数据集上的实验表明，在保持 ANNS 准确率的情况下，CS-PQ 相比当前先进的 CPU PQ 实现最高可实现 10.7 倍加速。

查看英文原文摘要

Product Quantization (PQ) construction is deeply integrated into vector index construction for Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search (ANNS). The rapid growth in vector dimensionality and volume has significantly increased the computational cost of PQ. Existing GPU-based PQ accelerations are ill-suited for PQ construction due to its "one-to-one" execution pattern (one compute, one data load, i.e., data transfer overhead dominates). Although CPU-based solutions are prevalent, they are essentially general-purpose designs that fail to capture the intrinsic characteristics of PQ construction.In this paper, we propose CS-PQ, a Cache-friendly, SIMD-optimized PQ framework based on modern CPUs. CS-PQ introduces a vector-oriented SIMD paradigm that decouples quantization granularity from SIMD width by vectorizing across PQ centroids rather than subvector dimensions. It further restructures the execution pipeline to improve cache locality and reformulates PQ computation to eliminate redundant operations while preserving correctness. Experiments on large-scale datasets show that CS-PQ achieves up to 10.7 times speedup over state-of-the-art CPU-based PQ implementations without sacrificing ANNS accuracy.

核心结论
CS-PQ 沿 PQ 质心进行 SIMD 向量化，并改善执行流水线的缓存局部性。在大规模数据集上，相比当前先进的 CPU PQ 实现，CS-PQ 在保持 ANNS 准确率的情况下最高实现 10.7 倍加速。
创新点
1. 沿 PQ 质心进行 SIMD 向量化，将量化粒度与 SIMD 宽度解耦；2. 重组执行流水线以改善缓存局部性；3. 改写 PQ 计算，在保持正确性的同时消除冗余操作。

Taking Cryptography Out of the Data Path via Near-Memory Processing in DRAM

arXiv 提交 2026-05-19 cs.CR cs.AR cs.DC 主题相关度 0.444

Nicola Barcarolo, Brahmaiah Gandham, Mohammad Sadrosadati, Roberto Passerone 等 6 人

中文摘要

AES-128 和 SHA-256 等密码算法是保障数据安全性和完整性的基础。尽管这些算法的计算效率较高，其性能却常受 CPU、GPU 等以处理器为中心的架构限制，主要原因是内存瓶颈。这一限制会增加延迟和能耗，在处理大量数据时尤其明显。为应对这些挑战，存内处理（Processing-in-Memory，PIM）成为一种有前景的架构范式，可直接在内存单元内部或其附近执行计算。PIM 通过尽量减少处理器与内存单元之间的数据搬移，可显著加速密码算法并提高能效。已有多项研究表明，PIM 能从架构层面有效加速密码算法，但尚无研究充分展示实际 PIM 系统的潜力。本文研究实际 PIM 系统加速密码算法的潜力和局限，并采用 UPMEM PIM 架构评估密码算法的可扩展性。当算法只在单个内存 rank 上运行时，性能仍低于现代 CPU。将计算分布到多个 rank 后，性能显著提高。利用所有可用 rank 时，实际 PIM 系统可以更有效地加速密码算法。

查看英文原文摘要

Cryptographic algorithms such as AES-128 and SHA-256 are fundamental to ensuring data security and integrity. Although these algorithms are computationally efficient, their performance is often constrained by the processor-centric architectures (e.g., CPUs, GPUs), primarily due to the memory bottleneck. This constraint leads to increased latency and higher energy consumption, particularly when handling large volumes of data. To overcome these challenges, Processing-in-Memory (PIM) has emerged as a promising architectural paradigm, allowing computation to occur directly within or near memory units. By minimizing data movement between the processor and memory units, PIM can significantly accelerate cryptographic algorithms while improving energy efficiency. Several pieces of prior work have demonstrated the effectiveness of PIM at fundamentally accelerating cryptographic algorithms. However, none of the prior works have extensively demonstrated the potential of a real-world PIM system. In this paper, we want to investigate the potential and limitations of real-world PIM in accelerating cryptographic algorithms. As part of our methodology, the UPMEM PIM architecture is used to assess the scalability of cryptographic algorithms. When these algorithms operate on a single rank, their performance remains below that of modern CPUs. However, distributing the computation across multiple ranks significantly enhances performance. When all available ranks are utilized, real-world PIM can accelerate cryptographic algorithms more effectively.

核心结论
在 UPMEM PIM 上，密码算法仅使用单个 rank 时的性能仍低于现代 CPU。将计算分布到多个 rank 可显著提高性能，利用全部可用 rank 时可实现更有效的加速。
创新点
1. 在实际 UPMEM PIM 平台上评估密码算法从单个 rank 扩展到多个 rank 的性能，考察其加速潜力与局限。

Distance Comparison Operations Are Not Silver Bullets in Vector Similarity Search: A Benchmark Study on Their Merits and Limits

arXiv 提交 2026-04-03 cs.DB 主题相关度 0.443

Zhuanglin Zheng, Yuxiang Zeng, Chenchen Liu, Yunzhen Chi 等 6 人

中文摘要

距离比较操作（Distance Comparison Operations，DCO）用于判断数据向量与查询向量之间的距离是否在给定阈值内，是向量相似性搜索的关键性能瓶颈。近期的 DCO 方法通过避免计算全维距离，有望显著加速搜索，但这些方法是否已适用于生产环境中的向量数据库系统，仍是一个开放问题。为此，我们在 10 个数据集上对 8 种 DCO 算法开展全面的基准测试，数据集规模最高达 1 亿个向量，维度最高达 12,288 维。测试涵盖多种硬件配置，包括使用和不使用 SIMD 的 CPU，以及 GPU。研究发现，这些方法的效率对数据维度高度敏感，遇到分布外查询时会下降，在不同硬件上的表现也不稳定。评估还揭示了这些方法常被忽视的优势，即它们可以加速索引构建和数据更新。尽管具有这些优势，其性能波动仍可能使它们慢于全维扫描。因此，我们认为，近期的 DCO 算法进展尚未达到生产部署的要求。

查看英文原文摘要

Distance Comparison Operations (DCOs), which decide whether the distance between a data vector and a query is within a threshold, are a critical performance bottleneck in vector similarity search. Recent DCO methods that avoid full-dimensional distance computations promise significant speedups, but their readiness for production vector database systems remains an open question. To address this, we conduct a comprehensive benchmark of 8 DCO algorithms across 10 datasets (with up to 100M vectors and 12,288 dimensions) and diverse hardware configurations (CPUs with/without SIMD, and GPUs). Our study reveals that these methods are not silver bullets: their efficiency is highly sensitive to data dimensionality, degrades under out-of-distribution queries, and is unstable across hardware. Yet, our evaluation also demonstrates often-overlooked merits: they can accelerate index construction and data updates. Despite these benefits, their unstable performance, which can be slower than a full-dimensional scan, leads us to conclude that recent algorithmic advancements in DCO are not yet ready for production deployment.

核心结论
对 8 种 DCO 算法的跨数据集和跨硬件测试表明，这些方法可加速索引构建和数据更新，但性能对维度敏感，遇到分布外查询时会下降，在不同硬件上也不稳定，有时慢于全维扫描。作者据此认为这些方法尚未达到生产部署的要求。
创新点
1. 在 10 个数据集上对 8 种 DCO 算法开展跨硬件基准测试，评估数据维度、分布外查询和硬件配置对性能的影响；2. 将评估范围扩展到索引构建和数据更新。

CompPow: A Case for Component-level GPU Power Management

arXiv 提交 2026-05-21 cs.AR cs.DC 主题相关度 0.443

Shaizeen Aga, Mohamed Assem Ibrahim

中文摘要

各领域对机器学习（ML）驱动的智能功能需求不断增长，推动了 GPU 的广泛应用。GPU 的功耗较高，在典型的机器学习数据中心中，GPU 往往占据功率预算的大部分。面向多块 GPU 的数据中心级功耗优化具有潜力，本文则着眼于单块 GPU 内部的功耗。现代 GPU 由多个集成组件构成，本文主张在功耗管理中考虑各组件的特性，并将这种方法称为 CompPow，以改进现代 GPU 的功耗管理。我们在多种机器学习操作和执行模式下表明，CompPow 有望将能效提高 10%，并将性能提高 5%。最后，本文就如何通过组件感知的软硬件协同设计进一步提高现代 GPU 的能效提出建议。

查看英文原文摘要

The ever increasing demand for ML-driven intelligence in a wide spectrum of domains has led to ubiquity of GPUs. At the same time, GPUs are notorious for their power consumption needs and often dominate power allocation in a typical ML datacenter. While datacenter-level power optimizations which focus on collection of GPUs are promising, in this work, we take a different tack -- namely, we take a closer look at power consumption inside a GPU. Specifically, as modern GPUs are comprised of integrated components, we make a case for component-awareness, termed CompPow in this work, for improved power management in modern GPUs. We demonstrate for a variety of ML operations and execution patterns, CompPow has the potential to deliver higher energy efficiency (10%) and even improved performance (5%). We conclude with recommendations on how component-aware software-hardware co-design can extract additional energy efficiency from modern GPUs.

核心结论
在多种机器学习操作和执行模式下，组件感知功耗管理 CompPow 有望将能效提高 10%、将性能提高 5%。摘要未说明这两项提升的比较基线。
创新点
1. 提出组件感知的 GPU 功耗管理方法 CompPow，将 GPU 内部组件的特性纳入功耗管理；2. 提出通过组件感知的软硬件协同设计进一步提高 GPU 能效的建议，摘要未说明具体机制。

Wattlytics: A Web Platform for Co-Optimizing Performance, Energy, and TCO in HPC Clusters

arXiv 提交 2026-04-09 cs.DC cs.AR cs.ET 主题相关度 0.443

Ayesha Afzal, Georg Hager, Gerhard Wellein

中文摘要

GPU 加速计算系统不断增长的算力需求和能耗，使设计和运维决策更难有据可依。本文发布 Wattlytics 的首个版本（https://wattlytics.netlify.app），这是一个基于浏览器的交互式决策支持系统。与现有面向采购的计算工具相比，Wattlytics 的独特之处在于，它将基于基准测试的 GPU 性能缩放、考虑动态电压与频率调节（DVFS）的分段功耗建模，以及多年期总拥有成本（TCO）分析集成到同一个交互环境中。用户可配置由当代 GPU（GH200、H100、L40S、L40、A40、A100 和 L4）组成的异构系统，选择 GROMACS、AMBER 等代表性科学计算负载，并在电价、系统寿命和频率调节等约束下探索部署方案。Wattlytics 计算多维决策指标，包括 TCO 构成、单位 TCO 完成的工作量、单位 TCO 对应的功率，以及按功率和 TCO 共同归一化的工作量。它还支持设计空间探索、假设情景分析、敏感性分析（弹性、Sobol 指数和蒙特卡洛方法）以及协作功能，以便在不确定条件下指导实际的集群设计与采购。我们选取若干部署情景，比较固定预算、固定 GPU 数量、固定性能和固定功率 4 种模式下的部署策略。案例研究表明，在预算或能耗受限时，经优化部署的高能效 GPU 在整体成本效益上可以优于性能更高的备选 GPU。Wattlytics 帮助用户探索设计参数空间，识别影响成本和风险的因素，使高性能计算（HPC）系统的设计决策更有依据，也更易解释。

查看英文原文摘要

The escalating computational demands and energy footprint of GPU-accelerated computing systems complicate informed design and operational decisions. We present the first release of Wattlytics (https://wattlytics.netlify.app), an interactive, browser-based decision-support system. Unlike existing procurement-oriented calculators, Wattlytics uniquely integrates benchmark-driven GPU performance scaling, dynamic voltage and frequency scaling (DVFS)-aware piecewise power modeling, and multi-year total cost of ownership (TCO) analysis within a single interactive environment. Users can configure heterogeneous systems across contemporary GPU architectures (GH200, H100, L40S, L40, A40, A100, and L4), select representative scientific workloads (e.g., GROMACS, AMBER), and explore deployment scenarios under constraints such as energy prices, system lifetime, and frequency scaling. Wattlytics computes multidimensional decision metrics (TCO breakdown, work-per-TCO, power-per-TCO, and work-per-watt-per-TCO) and supports design-space exploration, what-if scenarios, sensitivity metrics (elasticity, Sobol indices, Monte Carlo) and collaborative features to guide realistic cluster design and procurement under uncertainty. We demonstrate selected scenarios comparing deployment strategies under different operational modes: ixed budget, fixed GPU count, fixed performance, and fixed power. Our case studies show that, under budget or energy constraints, optimally deployed energy-efficient GPUs can outperform higher-performance alternatives in overall cost-effectiveness. Wattlytics helps users explore the design parameter space and distinguish between cost- and risk-driving factors, turning HPC design into a well-informed and explainable decision-making process.

核心结论
案例研究表明，在预算或能耗受限时，经优化部署的高能效 GPU 在整体成本效益上可以优于性能更高的备选 GPU。
创新点
1. 在同一个交互式浏览器平台中集成基于基准测试的 GPU 性能缩放、考虑 DVFS 的分段功耗建模和多年期 TCO 分析；2. 支持异构 GPU 集群配置以及固定预算、固定 GPU 数量、固定性能和固定功率 4 种模式下的部署比较；3. 提供多维决策指标、设计空间探索、假设情景分析、敏感性分析和协作功能。

Making Array-Based Translation Practical for Modern, High-Performance Buffer Management

arXiv 提交 2026-04-01 cs.DB 主题相关度 0.442

Xinjing Zhou, Jinming Hu, Andrew Pavlo, Michael Stonebraker

中文摘要

现代缓冲池必须支持比传统联机事务处理（OLTP）更广泛的工作负载组合。除了 B 树查找，数据库系统还越来越多地处理以扫描为主的分析负载，并服务于访问模式不规则、图遍历扇出较高的向量搜索索引。这些负载需要一种将逻辑页 ID 映射到驻留页帧的地址转换机制。该机制须在多种访问模式下均保持高效，可部署于用户空间，兼容大页，便于集成，同时由数据库管理系统（DBMS）控制页面淘汰和 I/O。现有设计只能满足其中部分要求。 本文提出 Calico，一个由 DBMS 控制、基于数组进行地址转换的实用缓冲池。基于数组的地址转换在几十年前就已提出，曾一度被弃用，如今借助现代硬件重新具备可行性。 Calico 将逻辑地址转换与操作系统页表解耦，使 DBMS 能够同时实现低开销地址转换、使用大页作为页帧的后备内存，以及细粒度页面管理。为使数组地址转换在采用大规模稀疏分层页标识符的 DBMS 中兼具实用性和高性能，Calico 引入 3 项技术，包括带路径缓存的多级地址转换、用于回收低频访问的转换表内存的打孔机制，以及利用并行性的分组预取。 我们针对扫描、OLTP 风格的 B 树访问和向量搜索开展评估。结果表明，在数据可全部驻留内存和数据超出内存容量两种情形下，Calico 的性能均达到或超过现有最先进方案。我们还将 Calico 实现为 PostgreSQL 缓冲区管理器的直接替代品，并与 pgvector 集成。在 PostgreSQL 上，向量搜索在数据可全部驻留内存时最高获得 3.9 倍加速，在数据超出内存容量时最高获得 6.5 倍加速，以扫描为主的查询最高获得 3 倍加速。

查看英文原文摘要

Modern buffer pools must now support a broader workload mix than classic OLTP alone. In addition to B-tree lookups, database systems increasingly serve scan-heavy analytics and vector-search indexes with irregular high-fan-out graph traversal access patterns. These workloads require a translation mechanism -- mapping logical page IDs to resident frames -- that is simultaneously fast across these diverse access patterns, deployable in user space,compatible with huge pages, easy to integrate, and still under DBMS control for eviction and I/O. Existing designs satisfy only subsets of these goals. This paper presents \textbf{\calico}, a practical DBMS-controlled buffer pool built around array-based translation, a decades-old-idea that was dissmissed but now viable with modern hardware. \calico decouples logical translation from OS page tables so that the DBMS can combine low-overhead translation with huge-page-backed frames and fine-grained page management. To make array translation practical and performant for DBMSes with large sparse hierarchical page identifiers, \calico introduces three techniques: multi-level translation with path caching, hole punching for reclaiming cold translation memory, and group prefetch to exploit parallelism. Our evaluation across scans, OLTP-style B-tree accesses, and vector search shows that \calico matches or outperforms the existing state-of-the-art in-memory and out-of-memory performance. We also implement \calico as a drop-in replacement for PostgreSQL's buffer manager and integrate it with \texttt{pgvector}. Across vector search, and scan-heavy workloads, \calico delivers up to 3.9$\times$ in-memory and 6.5$\times$ larger-than-memory speedup for PostgreSQL vector search, speeds up scan-heavy queries by up to 3$\times$.

核心结论
在扫描、OLTP 风格的 B 树访问和向量搜索评估中，Calico 的性能达到或超过现有最先进方案，涵盖数据可全部驻留内存和数据超出内存容量两种情形。相较于 PostgreSQL 原有缓冲区管理器，Calico 使 pgvector 向量搜索在两种情形下分别获得最高 3.9 倍和 6.5 倍加速，并使以扫描为主的查询最高加速 3 倍。
创新点
1. 构建由 DBMS 控制的数组式缓冲池，将逻辑地址转换与操作系统页表解耦，支持由大页提供后备内存的页帧和细粒度页面管理；2. 采用带路径缓存的多级地址转换，以支持大规模稀疏分层页标识符；3. 采用打孔机制回收低频访问的转换表内存；4. 采用分组预取利用并行性；5. 实现可直接替换 PostgreSQL 缓冲区管理器的版本并集成 pgvector。

与数据库系统和查询处理相关
